```yaml
  object: documented_trajectory_W
  origin_type: Trajectory
  source_reference: locked_PATH_trajectory_record_W

```

```text
Chapter 39 provisionally locked

≠ Constitutive Source Trace established

```

#### Integrated Chapter-39 completion decision

All §§39.1–39.14 are complete. All twenty-four assigned Chapter-39 Pressure Duties occur exactly once in canonical prose. The chapter preserves the lower and upper RETYPE boundaries, Functional Gain, Constitutive Source Trace, Type Integrity, Context Boundary, Counterfactual and Alternative Projection Tests, Analogy Boundary, Projection Elasticity, stable failure conditions, exact Loss, Stop, Non-Capture, canonical terminal routes, source preservation, failure continuity, and non-authority.

```text
complete local RETYPE boundary specification
+ twenty-four-duty closure

```

#### Chapter-40 handoff boundary

Chapter 40 receives the provisionally locked RETYPE procedure and family methods of Chapters 29–39. It must test them through positive cases, countercases, confusion cases, the thirty-two-point RETYPE Local Audit, canonical Output-Class mapping, and a closing lock decision without re-deriving earlier theory.

Chapter 40 receives ten locked constraints:

1. every case retains Source Object, origin type, source reference, Frame, granularity, relative levels, target context, target function, Claim Boundary, Counterfactual Sensitivity, exact Loss, alternatives, and no-projection;
2. positive cases do not validate RETYPE generally and must remain vulnerable to family-specific counterpressure;
3. countercases localize the exact failed claim without erasing valid Source material;
4. confusion cases separate `PROJECT_AS` from `COMPOSE`, `DECOMPOSE`, Φ recontextualization, structural analogy, repeated similarity, and modulator description;
5. compatible projections remain separate and competing projections may end in bounded preference, provisionality, partial admissibility, or Non-Capture;
6. `analogy_only`, Claim Reduction, Mandatory Stop, Failure, and Non-Capture remain reachable and non-equivalent;
7. origin type remains preserved in every admissible projection, and no macrofunction becomes a person or group type;
8. every case-level result maps to exactly one canonical Output Class only after the local audit;
9. smoke examples, prose sketches, schema validity, or formal completeness cannot substitute for required lock-critical case artifacts;
10. no case result changes PMS Base, Δ–Ψ, operation inventory, authority, legitimacy, recommendation, sanction, or governance.

Under the current production constraint, Chapter-40 preparation and prose drafting create no new test, case, or smoke YAML. Artifact obligations must remain explicit. An artifact-complete RETYPE lock may not be claimed unless the contractually required case files actually exist and pass the audit; where production remains deferred, the lock claim must remain correspondingly provisional.

<a id="chapter-39-completion-boundary"></a>

#### Chapter 39 completion boundary

Chapter 39 is provisionally locked at the local-boundary family-method level. The next controlled step is Chapter 40 WP1: §§40.1–40.8, covering Case Architecture and the seven positive RETYPE case families.

---

<a id="40-retype-cases-countercases-and-local-audit"></a>

## 40. RETYPE Cases, Countercases, and Local Audit

Chapter 40 applies the provisionally locked RETYPE method of Chapters 29–39 to explicit positive, negative, and confusion-case families. It does not derive another projection theory from examples. The cases test whether origin type, source reference, target coordinates, Constitutive Source Trace, PraxisPurchase, Counterfactual Sensitivity, alternatives, exact Loss, Claim Ceiling, Stop, Non-Capture, and canonical routing remain intact under concrete analytical pressure.

```text
case-family demonstration
≠ instantiated case artifact

instantiated case artifact
≠ general validation of RETYPE

local RETYPE audit
≠ integrated STRATA audit
```

Sections 40.1–40.8 establish the common case architecture and seven positive family demonstrations. Under the current production constraint, these sections create canonical prose only. They do not create the standalone Markdown/YAML/audit/mapping bundles required for artifact-complete RETYPE lock. Every missing artifact obligation therefore remains visible rather than being inferred from prose completeness, schema-valid smoke fixtures, or formal-model consistency.

<a id="40-1-case-architecture"></a>

### 40.1 Case Architecture

#### C40-CASEARCH-01 — every RETYPE case requires one source-locked, target-bounded, counterpressured packet with exact Loss, alternatives, local audit, and canonical mapping

A Chapter-40 case is not a narrative example with a PMS label attached. It is a delimited claim packet that preserves the source object and tests one or more explicit `PROJECT_AS` occurrences against the complete local RETYPE gate.

The minimum packet is:

```yaml
retype_case_packet:
  case_id:
  case_family:
  claim_id:
  artifact_status:
    canonical_prose:
    standalone_markdown_case:
    project_as_yaml_record:
    local_audit_record:
    canonical_mapping_record:
  source:
    object:
    origin_type:
    reference:
    frame:
    granularity:
    relative_level:
    temporal_scope:
    prior_disposition:
    uncertainty:
  target:
    context:
    object:
    level:
    frame:
    granularity:
    temporal_scope:
    function:
    claim_scope:
    validity_scope:
  relative_level_relation:
  constitutive_source_trace:
    features:
    relations:
    reconstruction_line:
    material_variations:
    irrelevant_variations:
  modulating_source_features:
  praxis_purchase:
  counterfactual_variations:
  alternatives:
    rival_function:
    narrower_claim:
    source_only:
    analogy_only:
    no_projection:
  claim_ceiling:
  failure_conditions:
  loss:
    preserved:
    compressed:
    excluded:
    uncertain:
    irrecoverable:
  local_audit_result:
  canonical_output_class:
  authority_inheritance: prohibited
```

Field presence is only a completeness burden. It does not prove that the source object exists as described, that the selected relations are constitutive, that the function changes target-side praxis, that a rival is weaker, or that a canonical class is warranted.

Chapter-40 case work therefore uses four distinct layers:

1. **Canonical family prose** — establishes the bounded claim, counterpressure, Loss, alternatives, and possible failure locations.
2. **Standalone Markdown case** — preserves the full narrative and evidence-facing reconstruction.
3. **YAML `PROJECT_AS` Record** — encodes the operation occurrence and closed formal fields.
4. **Local Audit and canonical mapping** — records the executed thirty-two-point audit and exactly one canonical Output Class for the delimited claim.

```text
Layer 1 complete
≠ Layers 2–4 complete

all fields present
≠ local audit passed

local audit passed
≠ empirical truth
≠ authority
```

WP1 produces only Layer 1 for `P1–P7`. The lock-critical artifact obligations remain deferred. Consequently, every positive family below keeps `local_audit_result: not_executed` and `canonical_output_class: not_selected`. “Positive” identifies the intended family pressure, not a preselected route.

A case may contain several operations only through separate claims and Records. A warranted `COMPOSE` output may later become the source of `PROJECT_AS`; a `DECOMPOSE` reconstruction may later support a different target function. Shared material does not merge operation identity, Loss, disposition, or canonical result.

```text
shared source history
≠ shared claim
≠ shared Record
≠ shared Loss
≠ shared result
```

#### C40-OCCURRENCEARTIFACT-01 — future lock-critical packages must expose concrete occurrence and relation traces through existing fields

Where a Chapter-40 case uses PMS-typed or PMS-derived source material, the standalone Markdown case and YAML `PROJECT_AS` Record must make the claim-relevant occurrence relations reconstructible through the existing source reference, source basis, Constitutive Source Trace, material variations, and five-part Loss. A bare operator label, a general Δ–Ψ list, or ordinary-language resemblance is not a substitute for occurrence identity, Frame, temporal placement, relation, and source support.

The later Local Audit must then test the delimited relation and map the executed claim to exactly one canonical Output Class. This requirement adds no `operator_trace` field, produces no missing artifact, and selects no result for `P1–P7`, `N1–N7`, or `X1–X6`.

```text
occurrence-level trace requirement documented
≠ lock-critical package produced
≠ case passed or failed
≠ artifact-complete RETYPE lock restored
```

<a id="40-2-case-1-trajectory-as-frame-function"></a>

### 40.2 Case 1: Trajectory as Frame-Function

#### C40-FRAMECASE-01 — test a Trajectory as bounded historical frame-function while retaining duration, expectations, costs, uncertainty, and non-determinism

The first positive family tests whether a completed PATH Trajectory can perform a bounded historical frame-function in a later coordination context without becoming a Frame operator or determining later action.

```yaml
case_P1_trajectory_as_frame_function:
  case_id: C40-P1
  source:
    object: documented_coordination_trajectory_R
    origin_type: Trajectory
    reference: locked_PATH_trajectory_record_R
    frame: coordination_practice_frame_R
    granularity: trajectory_granularity
    relative_level: source_level_L1
    temporal_scope: period_R
    prior_disposition: retained
    uncertainty:
      - disputed_boundary_between_phase_2_and_phase_3
      - incomplete_access_to_exit_cost_evidence
  target:
    context: later_coordination_context_C1
    object: role_specific_coordination_configuration_C1
    level: configuration_level_L2
    frame: later_coordination_frame_C1
    granularity: configuration_granularity
    temporal_scope: bounded_follow_on_period_C1
    function: bounded_historical_frame_function
    claim_scope: interpretation_expectation_and_coordination_cost_only
    validity_scope: declared_roles_and_period_only
  constitutive_source_trace:
    features:
      - repeated_non_decisions_under_supported_expectation
      - sedimented_role_expectations
      - accumulated_revision_and_exit_costs
      - documented_reversals_and_partial_repairs
    relations:
      - non_decisions_condition_later_credible_interpretations
      - accumulated_costs_condition_available_coordination_corridors
    reconstruction_line: source_history_changes_which_later_interpretations_and_coordination_moves_are_practically_credible
  praxis_purchase:
    - distinguishes_formally_available_from_practically_credible_options
    - distinguishes_equal_rule_text_from_unequal_role_specific_coordination_cost
  local_audit_result: not_executed
  canonical_output_class: not_selected
```

The proposed function is not “history matters.” It is the narrower claim that the retained trajectory changes which interpretations, expectations, and coordination moves are practically credible for declared roles in `C1`. The function therefore depends on sedimented expectations and costs, not on chronological priority alone.

The case must remain vulnerable to at least five counterpressures:

- **Present-condition sufficiency:** current rules, resources, or sanctions may explain the entire target difference without the trajectory.
- **Source removal:** if `R` is removed and the same interpretation/cost structure remains, Constitutive Source Trace fails.
- **Opposite-source pressure:** a materially different prior trajectory must not support the same bounded function unchanged.
- **Role variation:** the function may hold for one role relation and fail for another.
- **Non-determinism:** later actors may revise, contest, or exit the inherited frame.

```text
historical frame-function
≠ historical determinism
≠ causal monopoly
≠ origin-type replacement
```

The exact Loss must preserve the trajectory’s duration, phase order, supported Non-Events, expectations, costs, reversals, uncertainty, and prior disposition. It may compress source-internal detail not constitutive to the target claim; it must disclose excluded counterhistories, uncertain boundary evidence, and any irrecoverable source material.

Required alternatives remain open:

```yaml
P1_alternatives:
  rival_function: bounded_path_dependence_description_without_frame_function
  narrower_claim: one_role_specific_expectation_effect_in_one_subperiod
  source_only: trajectory_is_relevant_historical_background_only
  analogy_only: later_coordination_is_structurally_compared_to_prior_phase_order
  no_projection: present_target_conditions_explain_the_difference
```

The family demonstration shows what an admissible-frame candidate would have to carry. It does not establish that `R` performs the function, does not select a narrower claim, and does not map `C40-P1` to an Output Class.

<a id="40-3-case-2-trajectory-as-macro-event"></a>

### 40.3 Case 2: Trajectory as Macro-Event

#### C40-MACROCASE-01 — test a temporally extended Trajectory as Macro-Event without punctualization or loss of internal duration

The second positive family tests whether a temporally extended Trajectory can function as one bounded Macro-Event in a wider path while retaining its internal duration, phases, reversals, and uncertainty.

```yaml
case_P2_trajectory_as_macro_event:
  case_id: C40-P2
  source:
    object: documented_restructuring_trajectory_S
    origin_type: Trajectory
    reference: locked_PATH_trajectory_record_S
    frame: restructuring_practice_frame_S
    granularity: trajectory_granularity
    relative_level: source_level_L1
    temporal_scope: multi_phase_period_S
    internal_temporality:
      - initiation_phase
      - contested_implementation_phase
      - partial_reversal_phase
      - stabilization_phase
  target:
    context: wider_path_reconstruction_P2
    object: later_institutional_path_segment_P2
    level: path_level_L2
    frame: wider_path_frame_P2
    granularity: macro_event_granularity
    temporal_scope: wider_period_P2
    function: bounded_macro_event_function
    claim_scope: one_extended_turning_unit_with_internal_duration_preserved
    validity_scope: declared_path_and_period_only
  constitutive_source_trace:
    features:
      - extended_restructuring_sequence
      - contested_phase_transitions
      - partial_reversal
      - stabilization_with_residual_costs
    relations:
      - internal_phase_order_changes_later_path_branching
      - unresolved_residuals_condition_later_continuation
    reconstruction_line: the_extended_course_functions_as_one_larger_path_unit_only_because_its_internal_sequence_and_residuals_shape_later_branching
  local_audit_result: not_executed
  canonical_output_class: not_selected
```

The Macro-Event claim does not identify one decision date as the “real event.” It asks whether the temporally extended course can be treated as one higher-granularity unit for a specific wider-path reconstruction without erasing the source’s internal temporal structure.

```text
Macro-Event function
≠ punctual Event
≠ Event Cluster automatically
≠ narrative summary
```

The required counterpressure includes:

- punctualization to the formal initiation or completion date;
- fragmentation into unrelated Events;
- Event-Cluster sufficiency without one Macro-Event function;
- alternative periodization;
- removal of the reversal phase;
- current-path conditions that make the extended source unnecessary;
- a narrower claim restricted to one branch transition.

The Loss must preserve internal duration, contested transitions, reversal, stabilization, residual costs, uncertainty, and the source’s prior PATH disposition. Compression may reduce micro-detail but may not make the extended course appear instantaneous, fully coherent, or causally sufficient.

```yaml
P2_alternatives:
  rival_function: bounded_event_cluster_in_wider_path
  narrower_claim: one_phase_as_turning_event
  source_only: extended_trajectory_remains_background_history
  analogy_only: source_phase_structure_resembles_one_larger_event_form
  no_projection: later_path_branching_is_explained_by_present_conditions
```

The prose packet demonstrates the non-punctualized family burden. It does not establish a Macro-Event result or a canonical mapping.

<a id="40-4-case-3-recurrent-trajectory-form-as-attractor-function"></a>

### 40.4 Case 3: Recurrent Trajectory Form as Attractor-Function

#### C40-ATTRACTORCASE-01 — test recurrent trajectory form as dynamic attractor-function beyond repeated similarity or label recurrence

The third positive family tests whether several warrantable Trajectories instantiate one recurrent transition form whose practical load stabilizes later movement toward a bounded dynamic attractor-function.

```yaml
case_P3_recurrent_form_as_attractor_function:
  case_id: C40-P3
  source:
    object: recurrent_transition_form_T
    origin_type: derived_recurrent_form_composite
    reference:
      - locked_PATH_trajectory_record_T1
      - locked_PATH_trajectory_record_T2
      - locked_PATH_trajectory_record_T3
    frame: recurrent_coordination_frame_T
    granularity: recurrent_form_granularity
    relative_level: source_level_L1
    temporal_scope: bounded_recurrence_window_T
    recurrent_form:
      - temporary_exception
      - coordination_deferral
      - resource_lock_in
      - constrained_reopening
  target:
    context: later_transition_field_C3
    object: continuation_and_reopening_configuration_C3
    level: configuration_level_L2
    frame: later_transition_frame_C3
    granularity: configuration_granularity
    temporal_scope: bounded_follow_on_window_C3
    function: bounded_dynamic_attractor_function
    claim_scope: transition_access_and_persistence_only
    validity_scope: declared_configuration_and_roles_only
  constitutive_source_trace:
    features:
      - recurrent_transition_order
      - repeated_non_decision_at_deferral_position
      - accumulated_resource_lock_in
      - recurrently_narrowed_reopening_corridor
    relations:
      - deferral_and_lock_in_jointly_stabilize_one_transition_form
      - each_recurrence_changes_cost_of_departure_from_the_form
    reconstruction_line: later_transition_options_are_conditioned_by_the_recurrently_stabilized_course_form_not_by_visual_similarity_alone
  local_audit_result: not_executed
  canonical_output_class: not_selected
```

Repeated resemblance is insufficient. The case must show a common stabilizing load: recurring relations must affect access, friction, expectation, cost, or persistence in the later transition field.

```text
repeated similar course
≠ dynamic attractor-function

label recurrence
≠ stabilizing load

graph similarity
≠ praxeological recurrence
```

Counterpressure must vary:

- sequence order;
- the supported Non-Event at the deferral position;
- resource lock-in;
- one or more trajectories from the recurrent-form packet;
- a case with the same visual pattern but no changed transition cost;
- a case with different visible form but equivalent stabilizing load;
- present target conditions;
- no stable recurrent form.

The exact Loss must disclose which trajectory-specific features are compressed into the recurrent form, which countercourses are excluded, where recurrence boundaries remain uncertain, and what source detail cannot be recovered from the higher-level form.

```yaml
P3_alternatives:
  rival_function: bounded_recurrence_description_without_attractor_function
  narrower_claim: one_transition_cost_effect_in_one_role_relation
  source_only: recurrent_form_is_descriptive_source_structure_only
  analogy_only: trajectories_share_a_formal_sequence_pattern
  no_projection: later_transition_field_is_explained_by_current_lock_in_only
```

The prose packet demonstrates the additional burden beyond similarity. It does not establish that the recurrent form exists, carries stabilizing load, or warrants `PROJECT_AS`.

<a id="40-5-case-4-distributed-local-asymmetries-as-higher-level-function"></a>

### 40.5 Case 4: Distributed Local Asymmetries as Higher-Level Function

#### C40-HIGHERFUNCTIONCASE-01 — test distributed local asymmetries as a relationally formed higher-level function rather than aggregation

The fourth positive family begins only after a warranted `COMPOSE` occurrence has formed a relational composite from independently supported local asymmetry occurrences. The later higher-level function is a separate `PROJECT_AS` claim.

```yaml
case_P4_distributed_asymmetries_as_higher_level_function:
  case_id: C40-P4
  source:
    object: distributed_asymmetry_composite_U
    origin_type: derived_relational_composite
    reference: locked_COMPOSE_record_U
    frame: distributed_access_frame_U
    granularity: composite_granularity
    relative_level: source_level_L1
    temporal_scope: stabilization_window_U
    components:
      - access_asymmetry_u1
      - timing_asymmetry_u2
      - information_asymmetry_u3
      - revision_asymmetry_u4
    constitutive_relations:
      - u1_conditions_effect_of_u3
      - u2_delays_correction_of_u4
      - u3_and_u4_jointly_shift_revision_cost
  target:
    context: institutional_access_field_C4
    object: distributed_coordination_configuration_C4
    level: configuration_level_L2
    frame: institutional_access_frame_C4
    granularity: higher_level_function_granularity
    temporal_scope: bounded_continuation_window_C4
    function: bounded_higher_level_asymmetry_function
    claim_scope: access_revision_and_coordination_cost_only
    validity_scope: declared_configuration_and_period_only
  constitutive_source_trace:
    features:
      - independently_warranted_local_asymmetries
      - relation_topology
      - temporal_stabilization
      - accumulated_cross_component_effects
    reconstruction_line: the_target_difference_depends_on_the_relational_configuration_and_not_on_component_count_alone
  local_audit_result: not_executed
  canonical_output_class: not_selected
```

The case requires two separate records and two separate Loss accounts:

```text
local occurrences
→ COMPOSE
→ relational composite U

relational composite U
→ PROJECT_AS in C4
→ higher-level function candidate
```

```text
COMPOSE success
≠ PROJECT_AS success

many asymmetries
≠ higher-level function

higher-level visibility
≠ higher truth
≠ authority increase
```

Required counterpressure includes:

- component removal;
- component substitution;
- relation disruption;
- smaller-subset sufficiency;
- alternative composite boundaries;
- a count-matched aggregate without the relation topology;
- present target conditions;
- internal contradiction and countervailing components.

If a narrower subset carries all target work, the larger source may be overinclusive and Claim Reduction must remain available. If relation disruption leaves the same target claim unchanged, relational functional formation is not demonstrated.

```yaml
P4_alternatives:
  rival_function: bounded_coordination_cost_function_without_asymmetry_label
  narrower_claim: subset_u1_u3_only
  source_only: composite_is_descriptively_valid_but_functionally_inert
  analogy_only: local_asymmetries_share_a_distributed_pattern
  no_projection: current_target_rules_explain_access_and_revision_cost
```

The prose case does not assign component roles, a threshold result, a preferred subset, a target function, or a canonical class.

<a id="40-6-case-5-operator-weighting-profile-as-modulating-function"></a>

### 40.6 Case 5: Operator-Weighting Profile as Modulating Function

#### C40-PROFILECASE-01 — test a weighting profile as bounded modulating function without reordering Δ–Ψ, creating a primitive, or typing persons

The fifth positive family tests whether a warranted operator-weighting profile can perform a bounded modulating function in a later continuation context. The profile remains a configuration-bound relation among already warrantable operator occurrences and modulators.

```yaml
case_P5_weighting_profile_as_modulating_function:
  case_id: C40-P5
  source:
    object: operator_weighting_profile_V
    origin_type: configuration_bound_relational_composite
    reference: locked_profile_reconstruction_V
    frame: continuation_practice_frame_V
    granularity: profile_granularity
    relative_level: source_level_L1
    temporal_scope: bounded_profile_window_V
    operator_occurrences:
      omega_occurrence: relatively_prominent_in_access_and_cost_visibility
      theta_occurrence: relatively_prominent_in_threshold_and_timing
      remaining_dependencies: retained
    modulators:
      - access_condition_v1
      - timing_condition_v2
      - resource_constraint_v3
  target:
    context: later_continuation_field_C5
    object: role_specific_continuation_configuration_C5
    level: configuration_level_L2
    frame: later_continuation_frame_C5
    granularity: configuration_granularity
    temporal_scope: bounded_follow_on_window_C5
    function: bounded_continuation_cost_modulation_function
    claim_scope: access_threshold_timing_and_persistence_only
    validity_scope: declared_roles_phases_and_period_only
  constitutive_source_trace:
    features:
      - qualitative_relative_weighting
      - role_and_phase_variation
      - declared_modulators
      - preserved_operator_dependencies
    relations:
      - profile_conditions_access_and_threshold_visibility
      - modulators_shift_timing_and_persistence_without_becoming_operators
    reconstruction_line: later_continuation_cost_differs_only_if_the_declared_profile_and_modulator_relations_change_or_disappear
  local_audit_result: not_executed
  canonical_output_class: not_selected
```

The case may not rewrite prominence as operator independence, causal dominance, exclusivity, person trait, or a new primitive.

```text
relative weighting
≠ Δ–Ψ reordering

modulator
≠ PMS operator
≠ fourth STRATA operation

profile stability
≠ person or group type
≠ predictive power
```

Required counterpressure includes:

- operator-occurrence removal;
- role reversal;
- phase reversal;
- modulator removal, substitution, redundancy, and inversion;
- a rival profile;
- descriptive but functionally inert profile;
- no stable profile;
- present target conditions;
- no-projection.

The exact Loss must disclose compression of occurrence detail, excluded phases and roles, uncertainty in relative prominence, and any irrecoverable evidence about modulator timing. Dependencies may never be entered as “excluded” merely to simplify the profile.

```yaml
P5_alternatives:
  rival_function: bounded_threshold_visibility_function
  narrower_claim: one_role_one_phase_modulation
  source_only: profile_is_descriptively_valid_but_target_inert
  analogy_only: profile_structure_resembles_a_modulating_pattern
  no_projection: present_access_and_resource_conditions_explain_continuation_cost
```

The prose packet leaves profile formation, stability, modulation, target function, and output mapping open.

<a id="40-7-case-6-two-compatible-projections"></a>

### 40.7 Case 6: Two Compatible Projections

#### C40-COMPATIBLECASE-01 — test two projections in separated target coordinates without forced merger, contradiction, or authority increase

The sixth positive family uses one stable Source Object but two independently specified target claims.

```yaml
case_P6_two_compatible_projections:
  case_id: C40-P6
  shared_source:
    object: documented_trajectory_Z
    origin_type: Trajectory
    reference: locked_PATH_trajectory_record_Z
    frame: practice_frame_Z
    granularity: trajectory_granularity
    relative_level: source_level_L1
    temporal_scope: period_Z
    uncertainty: retained
    inherited_loss: retained
  candidate_A:
    target_context: later_configuration_A
    target_object: role_coordination_configuration_A
    target_level: configuration_level_L2
    target_frame: coordination_frame_A
    target_granularity: configuration_granularity
    temporal_scope: follow_on_period_A
    target_function: bounded_historical_frame_function
    claim_scope: credible_interpretations_and_role_expectations
  candidate_B:
    target_context: wider_path_B
    target_object: later_path_segment_B
    target_level: path_level_L3
    target_frame: wider_path_frame_B
    target_granularity: macro_event_granularity
    temporal_scope: wider_period_B
    target_function: bounded_macro_event_function
    claim_scope: one_extended_turning_unit_in_path_reconstruction
  local_audit_result: not_executed
  canonical_output_class: not_selected
```

The claims may be compatible because their target contexts, levels, objects, time windows, and PraxisPurchase differ. Compatibility is not inferred from shared source identity; it must be established by coordinate separation and non-conflicting bounded claims.

```text
same source
≠ same target coordinates

separate target coordinates
≠ both candidates admissible automatically

co-validity
≠ integrated super-function
```

Each candidate requires its own Constitutive Source Trace, Counterfactual Sensitivity, Loss, alternatives, failure conditions, local audit, and canonical mapping. The Loss profiles may differ because the frame-function and Macro-Event claims preserve different source relations.

Required counterpressure includes:

- coordinate overlap that would turn compatibility into competition;
- one candidate depending on the other without declaration;
- source-subset differences;
- a hybrid function that merely concatenates both labels;
- one candidate failing while the other survives;
- no-projection for one context;
- authority or comprehensiveness inflation from joint use.

```yaml
P6_alternatives:
  candidate_A_no_projection: present_configuration_conditions_only
  candidate_B_no_projection: event_cluster_or_source_only_path_description
  hybrid_candidate: prohibited_without_new_project_as_claim
  comparison_result: not_selected
```

The case-family prose leaves both candidate results and their compatibility status open. It does not merge them and does not assign one chapter-level class to an undifferentiated pair.

<a id="40-8-case-7-two-competing-projections"></a>

### 40.8 Case 7: Two Competing Projections

#### C40-COMPETINGCASE-01 — test competing projections in the same target coordinates with Source Trace, discrimination, Loss, bounded preference, provisionality, and Non-Capture open

The seventh positive family holds target coordinates stable while comparing two functions that claim materially overlapping explanatory work.

```yaml
case_P7_two_competing_projections:
  case_id: C40-P7
  shared_source:
    object: documented_trajectory_Y
    origin_type: Trajectory
    reference: locked_PATH_trajectory_record_Y
    frame: practice_frame_Y
    granularity: trajectory_granularity
    relative_level: source_level_L1
    temporal_scope: period_Y
    uncertainty: retained
    inherited_loss: retained
  shared_target_coordinates:
    context: continuation_field_C7
    object: later_transition_configuration_C7
    level: configuration_level_L2
    frame: continuation_frame_C7
    granularity: configuration_granularity
    temporal_scope: bounded_follow_on_period_C7
    claim_scope: transition_access_expectation_and_persistence
    validity_scope: declared_roles_and_period_only
  candidate_F:
    target_function: bounded_historical_frame_function
    proposed_purchase: changes_credible_interpretations_and_role_expectations
    candidate_specific_source_trace:
      - sedimented_expectations
      - accumulated_revision_costs
  candidate_G:
    target_function: bounded_dynamic_attractor_function
    proposed_purchase: changes_transition_access_and_persistence
    candidate_specific_source_trace:
      - recurrent_transition_form
      - accumulated_departure_friction
  local_audit_result: not_executed
  canonical_output_class: not_selected
```

Competition requires more than different vocabulary. `F` and `G` must overlap in target object, level, temporal scope, and practical work. Each must first satisfy its own family conditions. Only then may the comparison test Source Trace, discrimination, assumptions, exact Loss, Context Fit, Type Integrity, Claim Ceiling, and Parsimony.

```text
different function names
≠ competition

candidate F fails
≠ candidate G succeeds automatically

narrower candidate
≠ weaker candidate automatically
```

The comparison must preserve at least five live outcomes:

1. bounded preference for `F`;
2. bounded preference for `G`;
3. both remain provisionally supported under different delimited subclaims;
4. neither exceeds source-only or no-projection;
5. responsible discrimination is unavailable and Non-Capture remains required.

Counterpressure includes:

- candidate-specific source-subset changes;
- removal of sedimented expectations;
- disruption of recurrent form;
- present target conditions;
- narrower role- or phase-specific claims;
- candidate-specific countercases;
- different Loss profiles;
- an overbroad hybrid candidate;
- a source-only rival.

```yaml
P7_comparison_space:
  bounded_preference_F: open
  bounded_preference_G: open
  provisional_dual_support: open
  partially_admissible_segmentation: open
  source_only_or_no_projection: open
  non_capture: open
```

No numerical ranking, compensatory score, theory tribunal, or authority increase is permitted. A bounded comparative preference, if later warranted, would remain a claim-specific result and would not identify the Source Object’s “true type.”

#### WP1 completion boundary

WP1 establishes the common case packet and seven positive family demonstrations. All eight assigned WP1 duties occur exactly once in canonical prose. Each family retains source identity, target coordinates, Constitutive Source Trace burden, PraxisPurchase, counterfactuals, alternatives, exact Loss, Claim Ceiling, failure conditions, operation separation, canonical routing discipline, and non-authority.

The seven families remain deliberately unadjudicated:

```yaml
chapter_40_wp1_results:
  P1_trajectory_as_frame_function: not_selected
  P2_trajectory_as_macro_event: not_selected
  P3_recurrent_form_as_attractor_function: not_selected
  P4_distributed_asymmetries_as_higher_level_function: not_selected
  P5_weighting_profile_as_modulating_function: not_selected
  P6_two_compatible_projections: not_selected
  P7_two_competing_projections: not_selected
  local_audit_results: not_executed
  canonical_output_classes: not_selected
  lock_critical_case_artifacts: not_produced
```

```text
positive family prose complete
≠ positive case adjudicated
≠ lock-critical case artifact complete
≠ RETYPE lock
```

WP2 receives the stable case architecture and positive-family pressure packets. It must test the seven countercase families without converting every error into the same route, without erasing valid source material, and without claiming that prose-level demonstration substitutes for required standalone case artifacts.

<a id="40-9-countercase-1-origin-type-replacement"></a>

### 40.9 Countercase 1: Origin-Type Replacement

#### C40-ORIGINTYPECOUNTER-01 — localize origin-type replacement without invalidating the source object or every bounded target-function candidate

The first countercase begins from a warranted PATH Trajectory and a target-function claim that has been rewritten as source identity.

```yaml
countercase_N1_origin_type_replacement:
  case_id: C40-N1
  artifact_status:
    canonical_prose: complete
    standalone_markdown_case: not_produced
    project_as_yaml_record: not_produced
    local_audit_record: not_produced
    canonical_mapping_record: not_produced
  source:
    object: documented_trajectory_N1
    origin_type: Trajectory
    reference: locked_PATH_trajectory_record_N1
    frame: declared_practice_frame_N1
    granularity: trajectory_granularity
    relative_level: source_level_L1
    temporal_scope: period_N1
    prior_disposition: retained
    uncertainty: retained
  bounded_candidate:
    target_context: later_coordination_context_C_N1
    target_object: role_coordination_configuration_C_N1
    target_level: configuration_level_L2
    target_function: bounded_historical_frame_function
    origin_type_preserved: true
  overstrong_claim:
    statement: documented_trajectory_N1_is_a_Frame_operator
    origin_type_after_claim: Frame
    pressure: target_function_overwrites_origin_type
  claim_ceiling: bounded_target_role_without_source_identity_revision
  failure_conditions:
    - target_function_replaces_origin_type
    - source_reference_or_prior_disposition_erased
  loss:
    preserved: source_identity_and_bounded_candidate_distinction
    compressed: target_role_language
    excluded: source_target_type_identity
    uncertain: bounded_function_result_not_adjudicated
    irrecoverable: none_declared_at_layer_1
  local_audit_result: not_executed
  canonical_output_class: not_selected
```

The invalidity pressure lies at the identity claim, not at every adjacent proposition.

```text
Trajectory N1 functions as bounded historical frame in C_N1
≠ Trajectory N1 is a Frame operator

invalid identity claim
≠ source Trajectory invalid
≠ bounded function impossible in every context
```

The case must preserve:

- the original PATH source reference and disposition;
- the source origin type before and after any valid projection;
- the distinction between target role and source identity;
- the possibility of a separately specified bounded function;
- the full five-part Loss for each new claim;
- the failed stronger claim if a narrower claim is later tested.

Required counterpressure includes label removal, replacement of “is” with a fully specified “functions as,” target-context variation, source removal, and comparison with a pure metaphor or structural analogy. Rewriting the sentence does not repair the original identity claim. A reduced claim would require a new `PROJECT_AS` occurrence and a retained prior disposition.

```yaml
N1_alternatives:
  narrower_claim: trajectory_N1_functions_as_bounded_frame_in_C_N1
  source_only: trajectory_N1_remains_relevant_history_without_target_function
  analogy_only: trajectory_N1_structurally_resembles_a_frame_relation
  no_projection: present_coordination_conditions_explain_target_difference
```

The prose packet does not select Invalid Type Jump, Claim Reduction, Failure, Stop, or another route.

<a id="40-10-countercase-2-projection-without-context"></a>

### 40.10 Countercase 2: Projection without Context

#### C40-NOCONTEXTCOUNTER-01 — distinguish missing target coordinates from a substantively failed, adequately specified projection

The second countercase uses a strong functional label whose target coordinates are absent.

```yaml
countercase_N2_projection_without_context:
  case_id: C40-N2
  artifact_status:
    canonical_prose: complete
    standalone_markdown_case: not_produced
    project_as_yaml_record: not_produced
    local_audit_record: not_produced
    canonical_mapping_record: not_produced
  source:
    object: documented_trajectory_N2
    origin_type: Trajectory
    reference: locked_PATH_trajectory_record_N2
    frame: declared_practice_frame_N2
    granularity: trajectory_granularity
    relative_level: source_level_L1
    temporal_scope: period_N2
    prior_disposition: retained
    uncertainty: retained
  proposed_statement: trajectory_N2_is_an_attractor
  target_coordinates:
    context: absent
    object: absent
    level: absent
    frame: absent
    granularity: absent
    temporal_scope: absent
    function: dynamic_attractor_label_only
    claim_scope: absent
    validity_scope: absent
  source_trace: undeclared
  counterfactual_sensitivity: untested
  claim_ceiling: no_formal_projection_result_without_complete_coordinates
  failure_conditions:
    - target_coordinates_remain_missing
    - missing_fields_are_filled_by_inference_or_label
  loss:
    preserved: source_statement_and_coordinate_gaps
    compressed: undeclared_target_intention
    excluded: guessed_context_function_and_scope
    uncertain: intended_claim_form
    irrecoverable: none_declared_at_layer_1
  local_audit_result: not_executed
  canonical_output_class: not_selected
```

Missing coordinates prevent responsible adjudication of the formal projection claim. They do not establish that an adequately specified attractor-function claim would fail.

```text
target coordinates absent
→ formal PROJECT_AS occurrence incomplete

complete target coordinates
+ family conditions fail
→ substantive projection failure pressure
```

The case must not infer a negative target function from missing information. It must also not fill missing fields by guessing from the label, source domain, analyst intention, model similarity, or familiar examples.

A complete re-entry would need at least:

```yaml
required_target_coordinates:
  target_context:
  target_object:
  target_level:
  target_frame:
  target_granularity:
  temporal_scope:
  target_function:
  claim_scope:
  validity_scope:
  affected_praxis_dimensions:
```

Context completion is a new testable claim. It does not retroactively validate the underspecified statement, establish Source Trace, or select an output.

```yaml
N2_alternatives:
  bounded_metaphor: trajectory_N2_acts_like_an_attractor_here
  structural_analogy: recurrent_transition_form_similarity_only
  source_only: trajectory_N2_described_without_target_function
  no_projection: target_conditions_sufficient
  non_capture: intended_claim_form_cannot_be_responsibly_determined
```

The prose packet leaves formal incompleteness, substantive failure, metaphor, analogy, Claim Reduction, Stop, and Non-Capture distinct.

<a id="40-11-countercase-3-label-substitution"></a>

### 40.11 Countercase 3: Label Substitution

#### C40-LABELSUBCOUNTER-01 — test label substitution through absent Source Trace, Counterfactual Sensitivity, PraxisPurchase, Loss, and rivals

The third countercase attaches a familiar PMS-derived label to a target-domain pattern without the burden required for either analogy or projection.

```yaml
countercase_N3_label_substitution:
  case_id: C40-N3
  artifact_status:
    canonical_prose: complete
    standalone_markdown_case: not_produced
    project_as_yaml_record: not_produced
    local_audit_record: not_produced
    canonical_mapping_record: not_produced
  target:
    object: repeated_workflow_trace_N3
    domain: technical_workflow_domain_N3
    observed_pattern: repeated_return_to_one_state_sequence
  assigned_label: dynamic_attractor
  target_context: undeclared
  target_function: metaphorical_or_rhetorical_only
  source_object: absent
  source_trace: absent
  praxis_purchase: undeclared
  counterfactual_sensitivity: untested
  serious_rivals: absent
  claim_ceiling: target_pattern_description_without_pms_function_claim
  failure_conditions:
    - label_replaces_source_trace_and_praxis_purchase
    - label_survives_material_variation_without_discrimination
  loss:
    preserved: undeclared
    compressed: undeclared
    excluded: undeclared
    uncertain: undeclared
    irrecoverable: undeclared
  local_audit_result: not_executed
  canonical_output_class: not_selected
```

The label does no additional analytical work merely because the trace repeats.

```text
PMS vocabulary present
≠ PMS relation established

repetition
≠ attractor-function

rhetorical coherence
≠ PraxisPurchase
```

The case tests whether removal of the label changes any reconstruction, action corridor, expectation, threshold, cost, or countercase boundary. It must also vary the repeated sequence, target context, execution rules, resource constraints, and ordinary domain description.

Substitution pressure rises where:

- the label survives every material target change;
- no source-dependent relation can be reconstructed;
- the same term covers visualization, execution, recurrence, persistence, and explanation without distinction;
- Loss remains empty or fictitious;
- ordinary workflow-language explains the same pattern;
- additional PMS labels are added after objections without new test load.

```yaml
N3_alternatives:
  ordinary_domain_description: repeated_state_sequence_under_fixed_transition_rules
  bounded_metaphor: attractor_like_recurrence
  structural_analogy: recurrent_form_correspondence_with_residuals
  no_label: retain_target_description_only
  non_capture: semantic_relation_cannot_be_responsibly_adjudicated
```

The existing canonical routes remain open. This prose packet does not automatically map substitution pressure to `failed_transformation`; the executed claim, continuation burden, possible bounded analogy, and responsible adjudicability still require separate audit.

<a id="40-12-countercase-4-analogy-presented-as-projection"></a>

### 40.12 Countercase 4: Analogy Presented as Projection

#### C40-ANALOGYCOUNTER-01 — reduce formal or cross-domain resemblance to `analogy_only` where Semantic Preservation or Target Function remains unwarranted

The fourth countercase begins with a genuine formal correspondence and then overstates it as a source-dependent target function.

```yaml
countercase_N4_analogy_presented_as_projection:
  case_id: C40-N4
  artifact_status:
    canonical_prose: complete
    standalone_markdown_case: not_produced
    project_as_yaml_record: not_produced
    local_audit_record: not_produced
    canonical_mapping_record: not_produced
  source:
    object: documented_transition_trajectory_N4
    origin_type: Trajectory
    domain: historical_praxis_domain_N4
    reference: locked_PATH_trajectory_record_N4
  target:
    object: executable_state_transition_trace_N4
    domain: technical_workflow_domain_N4
  warranted_correspondence_candidates:
    - transition_phase_to_state_sequence
    - branch_point_to_branch_node
    - reversal_to_rollback_transition
  semantic_preservation: unestablished
  proposed_target_function: historical_trajectory_governs_workflow_execution
  source_only_residuals:
    - expectation
    - responsibility
    - binding_and_exit_costs
    - warranted_non_events
  target_only_residuals:
    - executable_guards
    - runtime_constraints
    - machine_failure_semantics
  claim_ceiling: bounded_formal_or_structural_correspondence_only
  failure_conditions:
    - formal_similarity_is_used_as_semantic_preservation
    - executable_mapping_is_used_as_target_function_warrant
  loss:
    preserved: declared_sequence_branch_and_reversal_correspondences
    compressed: source_and_target_internal_detail
    excluded: unsupported_semantic_identity_and_target_function
    uncertain: degree_of_praxeological_semantic_preservation
    irrecoverable: non_mappable_source_and_target_residuals_if_suppressed
  local_audit_result: not_executed
  canonical_output_class: not_selected
```

Formal and executable correspondence may remain useful while Semantic Preservation and Target Function fail.

```text
formal relation preserved
≠ praxeological semantic role preserved

executable mapping
≠ valid PROJECT_AS

projection claim fails
≠ analogy fails automatically
```

The case requires source-feature removal, target-feature removal, reverse mapping, ordinary-domain alternatives, source-only and target-only residuals, incompatibility disclosure, and no-projection. It must test whether the proposed function adds anything beyond the explicitly bounded correspondence.

Where only a delimited relation survives, a later executed audit may support `analogy_only` or Claim Reduction. That possibility is not an automatic routing rule and cannot be selected before the full claim and residual burdens are reviewed.

```yaml
N4_alternatives:
  partial_analogy: sequence_branch_and_reversal_correspondence_only
  narrower_claim: one_declared_relation_under_one_comparison_dimension
  source_only: preserve_historical_trajectory_without_target_function
  target_only: describe_workflow_execution_in_native_terms
  no_projection: no_source_dependent_target_function
```

The source remains a Trajectory; the target does not become a Trajectory by label, and no cross-domain mapping creates a primitive or authority.

<a id="40-13-countercase-5-macrofunction-from-mere-aggregation"></a>

### 40.13 Countercase 5: Macrofunction from Mere Aggregation

#### C40-AGGREGATIONCOUNTER-01 — reject macrofunction claims based on count, repetition, density, salience, or aggregation without relational formation

The fifth countercase collects many warranted local occurrences and infers a higher-level function from quantity alone.

```yaml
countercase_N5_macrofunction_from_aggregation:
  case_id: C40-N5
  artifact_status:
    canonical_prose: complete
    standalone_markdown_case: not_produced
    compose_yaml_record: not_produced
    project_as_yaml_record: not_produced
    local_audit_record: not_produced
    canonical_mapping_record: not_produced
  source_occurrences:
    - local_asymmetry_N5_A1
    - local_asymmetry_N5_A2
    - local_asymmetry_N5_A3
    - local_asymmetry_N5_A4
  declared_composite:
    object: asymmetry_collection_N5
    formation_basis:
      count: high
      repetition: repeated
      density: high
      salience: high
      relation_topology: undeclared
      constitutive_relations: undeclared
  proposed_target:
    context: institutional_access_field_N5
    function: bounded_higher_level_asymmetry_function
  functional_formation_threshold: unestablished
  claim_ceiling: descriptive_aggregation_until_relational_formation_is_warranted
  failure_conditions:
    - count_or_salience_substitutes_for_relation_topology
    - higher_level_function_survives_relation_disruption_unchanged
  loss:
    preserved: warranted_local_occurrences
    compressed: local_occurrence_detail_in_collection_view
    excluded: unsupported_constitutive_relations_and_macrofunction
    uncertain: composite_boundary_and_threshold
    irrecoverable: relation_history_if_not_recorded
  local_audit_result: not_executed
  canonical_output_class: not_selected
```

The source occurrences may all be valid while the composite and target-function claims remain unsupported.

```text
many warranted components
≠ warranted composite

warranted composite
≠ higher-level function automatically

count + repetition + density + salience
≠ relational functional formation
```

The case must test:

- relation disruption while count remains fixed;
- a count-matched random collection;
- component removal and replacement;
- Smaller-Subset Sufficiency;
- alternative composite boundaries;
- target-context variation;
- aggregation as a legitimate weaker result;
- internal countervailing relations;
- present target conditions;
- no-projection.

A valid `COMPOSE` occurrence, where warranted, must precede and remain separate from any later `PROJECT_AS` occurrence. Each retains its own Loss and disposition.

```yaml
N5_alternatives:
  descriptive_aggregation: warranted_local_occurrences_without_macrofunction
  narrower_composite: relation_supported_subset_only
  rival_function: bounded_distribution_visibility_function
  source_only: local_asymmetries_remain_separately_relevant
  no_projection: target_access_pattern_explained_by_present_rules
```

The packet leaves composite formation, threshold passage, Claim Reduction, Failure, Stop, and other routes unselected.

<a id="40-14-countercase-6-projection-as-claim-rescue"></a>

### 40.14 Countercase 6: Projection as Claim Rescue

#### C40-RESCUECOUNTER-01 — preserve the failed earlier disposition when a new level, context, label, function, or source subset is introduced

The sixth countercase begins with a delimited failed claim and then relocates it rather than answering its counterexample.

```yaml
countercase_N6_projection_as_claim_rescue:
  case_id: C40-N6
  artifact_status:
    canonical_prose: complete
    standalone_markdown_case: not_produced
    project_as_yaml_record: not_produced
    local_audit_record: not_produced
    canonical_mapping_record: not_produced
  prior_claim:
    claim_id: N6_original_claim
    statement: trajectory_N6_performs_bounded_frame_function_in_C0
    disposition: failed_transformation_candidate_not_selected
    failure_pressure: source_removal_left_target_difference_unchanged
  rescue_sequence:
    - move_to_higher_level_L3
    - rename_function_macro_event_attractor_profile
    - widen_target_context_C1_to_C4
    - narrow_source_subset_without_registering_new_claim
    - omit_original_counterexample
  failure_continuity: violated_candidate
  new_claim_records: absent
  claim_ceiling: prior_failure_retained_and_any_new_claim_independently_bounded
  failure_conditions:
    - prior_counterexample_is_omitted_or_relocated
    - changed_coordinates_are_not_registered_as_new_claim
  loss:
    preserved: original_claim_disposition_and_counterexample
    compressed: rescue_sequence_description
    excluded: retroactive_repair_of_original_claim
    uncertain: whether_any_new_bounded_claim_is_independently_warranted
    irrecoverable: failure_history_if_prior_disposition_is_deleted
  local_audit_result: not_executed
  canonical_output_class: not_selected
```

A changed coordinate or function may support a new claim, but it cannot erase the earlier failure.

```text
new level
≠ earlier objection answered

new function label
≠ new warrant

new source subset
≠ old claim repaired
```

Failure-preserving re-entry requires:

- the original claim ID and disposition;
- explicit declaration of every changed coordinate;
- a new `PROJECT_AS` occurrence;
- an independently warranted Source Trace;
- fresh counterfactuals and alternatives;
- a new exact Loss;
- stable failure conditions;
- no claim that the new result retroactively changes the old one.

The case must distinguish legitimate Claim Reduction from endless narrowing. A reduced claim explicitly surrenders the broader claim and independently tests the smaller one. Repeated relocation that preserves the strongest wording while moving every failure condition creates Mandatory-Stop pressure.

```yaml
N6_alternatives:
  failure_preserving_reentry: new_bounded_claim_with_prior_disposition_retained
  claim_reduction: explicitly_surrender_broader_claim_and_test_narrower_claim
  source_only: retain_valid_source_reconstruction_without_projection
  no_projection: preserve_original_failure
  non_capture: new_claim_cannot_be_responsibly_distinguished_from_rescue
```

No route is selected in Layer 1. The earlier failure pressure remains visible regardless of any later candidate.

<a id="40-15-countercase-7-person-level-type-jump"></a>

### 40.15 Countercase 7: Person-Level Type Jump

#### C40-PERSONJUMPCOUNTER-01 — prohibit conversion of configuration or composite function into person/group essence, diagnosis, rank, motive, or sanction basis

The seventh countercase begins with a configuration-level function and attributes it to a person or group as an intrinsic property.

```yaml
countercase_N7_person_level_type_jump:
  case_id: C40-N7
  artifact_status:
    canonical_prose: complete
    standalone_markdown_case: not_produced
    project_as_yaml_record: not_produced
    local_audit_record: not_produced
    canonical_mapping_record: not_produced
  source_configuration:
    object: relational_configuration_N7
    origin_type: derived_relational_composite
    reference: warranted_configuration_record_N7
    level: configuration_level_L2
    proposed_function: bounded_binding_or_asymmetry_function
  person_relation:
    person: role_holder_p_N7
    warranted_relation_candidates:
      - participates_in_configuration
      - occupies_declared_role
      - may_reproduce_resist_or_be_constrained_by_function
  overstrong_claim:
    statement: role_holder_p_N7_is_the_binding_or_asymmetry_type
    prohibited_inferences:
      - essence
      - diagnosis
      - stable_trait
      - motive
      - moral_rank
      - legitimacy
      - recommendation
      - sanction
  claim_ceiling: bounded_role_and_participation_relation_only
  failure_conditions:
    - configuration_function_is_predicated_as_person_or_group_essence
    - structural_relation_is_used_for_diagnosis_rank_motive_or_sanction
  loss:
    preserved: configuration_level_function_and_bounded_role_relations
    compressed: role_history_and_context_detail
    excluded: person_or_group_essence_diagnosis_rank_motive_and_sanction
    uncertain: role_specific_contribution_and_resistance
    irrecoverable: contextual_role_history_if_collapsed_into_trait
  local_audit_result: not_executed
  canonical_output_class: not_selected
```

The level and subject of predication must remain explicit.

```text
configuration performs function
≠ person owns function

person participates in relation
≠ person embodies macrofunction

role position
≠ diagnosis
≠ motive
≠ moral rank
```

The case must distinguish bounded participation claims from person typing. A person may contribute to, reproduce, resist, exploit, repair, or be constrained by a structure. Each is a separate evidence-bearing relation with its own temporal and contextual scope.

Removal or replacement of the person, role change, configuration dissolution, and cross-context comparison must be tested. If the alleged person property remains unchanged despite configuration removal, the claim is no longer a configuration-function projection and requires an independently warranted theory outside STRATA’s authority.

```yaml
N7_alternatives:
  bounded_role_relation: person_p_participates_in_configuration_N7_under_declared_scope
  configuration_only: retain_function_at_configuration_level
  source_only: describe_role_and_relations_without_target_person_function
  no_person_claim: no_projection_to_person_or_group
  mandatory_stop_pressure: continuation_requires_diagnosis_rank_or_sanction
```

The packet does not adjudicate the configuration function, the person relation, a route, or an Output Class. It preserves the categorical prohibition on authority inheritance.

<a id="chapter-40-wp2-completion-boundary"></a>

#### WP2 completion boundary

#### C40-COUNTERROUTING-01 — keep Claim Reduction, Mandatory Stop, Failure, Analogy-only, and Non-Capture available under their distinct burdens rather than one automatic countercase route

WP2 establishes seven negative family demonstrations, not seven preselected failures. Each packet identifies an intended invalidity pressure while retaining the exact point at which the claim may be incomplete, overstrong, reducible, analogous, prohibited to continue, substantively failed, or responsibly unadjudicable.

```text
countercase polarity
≠ automatic failed_transformation

local defect
≠ one universal route

stronger claim fails
≠ source object erased
≠ every narrower claim fails
```

The route burdens remain distinct:

- `claim_reduction_required` requires an independently warranted narrower claim and explicit surrender of the broader one;
- `mandatory_stop` requires that continuation would depend on source invention, type or person jump, failure erasure, primitive/authority inflation, or repeated anti-immunization violation;
- `failed_transformation` requires an adequately specified and executed delimited claim that fails its required conditions;
- `analogy_only` requires a useful bounded correspondence without warranted Semantic Preservation or Target Function;
- `non_capture` requires that responsible adjudication among materially non-equivalent claims is unavailable without invention.

The seven countercase families remain unadjudicated:

```yaml
chapter_40_wp2_results:
```

All sixteen cumulative Chapter-40 duties from WP1–WP2 occur exactly once in canonical prose. No confusion-case duty is advanced. WP3 receives the common case architecture, seven positive families, and seven countercase families. It must test the six confusion families while preserving separate operation records, semantic claim forms, Loss, failure continuity, route openness, and the prohibition on invented lock-critical artifacts.

<a id="40-16-confusion-case-1-retype-or-sub"></a>

### 40.16 Confusion Case 1: RETYPE or SUB?

#### C40-SUBCONFUSION-01 — require separate `DECOMPOSE` and `PROJECT_AS` records where source-internal opening and target-function assignment both occur

This confusion case begins with one provisionally compressed source occurrence whose internal organization must be opened before any target-function claim can be evaluated. The analytical pressure is not whether one operation can be renamed as the other. It is whether two non-equivalent questions are being answered:

```text
What finer source structure belongs to the same reference object?
→ DECOMPOSE

What bounded function may the retained origin-typed source perform in a declared target context?
→ PROJECT_AS
```

```yaml
confusion_case_X1_retype_or_sub:
  case_id: C40-X1
  artifact_status:
    canonical_prose: complete
    standalone_markdown_case: not_produced
    decompose_yaml_record: not_produced
    project_as_yaml_record: not_produced
    local_audit_record: not_produced
    canonical_mapping_record: not_produced
  source_entry:
    object: compressed_trajectory_occurrence_X1
    origin_type: Trajectory
    reference: locked_source_occurrence_X1
    granularity: coarse_trajectory_granularity
    prior_disposition: retained
  decompose_candidate:
    question: whether_internal_phases_relations_and_reversals_change_the_reconstruction_of_the_same_trajectory
    source_reference_retained: true
    proposed_finer_reconstruction:
      - phase_X1_a
      - phase_X1_b
      - reversal_X1_r
    result: unadjudicated
  project_as_candidate:
    source_object: compressed_trajectory_occurrence_X1_or_warranted_finer_reconstruction
    target_context: later_coordination_context_X1
    target_function: bounded_historical_frame_function
    origin_type_preserved: true
    result: unadjudicated
  claim_ceiling: separate_source_resolution_and_target_function_claims_only
  failure_conditions:
    - one_record_claims_to_both_open_source_and_assign_target_function
    - finer_resolution_is_treated_as_higher_truth
    - decompose_result_is_assumed_to_establish_project_as
    - project_as_is_used_to_invent_missing_source_components
  loss:
    preserved: source_reference_origin_type_and_separate_operation_lineage
    compressed: operation_specific_detail_in_family_demonstration
    excluded: automatic_invertibility_and_result_inheritance
    uncertain: whether_finer_reconstruction_or_target_function_is_warranted
    irrecoverable: source_detail_not_recorded_before_initial_compression
  local_audit_result: not_executed
  canonical_output_class: not_selected
```

The two records may form a controlled sequence, but their results and Loss remain independent:

```text
DECOMPOSE succeeds
≠ PROJECT_AS succeeds

PROJECT_AS succeeds
≠ finer reconstruction was necessary

finer reconstruction available
≠ finer reconstruction has truth priority
```

A direct `PROJECT_AS` claim remains possible where the coarse source already carries sufficient trace. Conversely, `DECOMPOSE` may be warranted while no target function exists. If target-function language is doing the work of invented source reconstruction, the correct response is not to merge the operations but to localize the unsupported source and projection claims.

```yaml
X1_alternatives:
  decompose_only: finer_source_reconstruction_without_target_function
  project_as_only: coarse_source_sufficient_for_bounded_target_claim
  two_record_sequence: decompose_then_independent_project_as
  resolution_neutral: finer_opening_changes_no_relevant_reconstruction
  no_projection: source_reconstruction_retained_without_target_function
  non_capture: operation_order_or_required_source_resolution_not_responsibly_decidable
```

No operation order, source resolution, function, route, or Output Class is selected.

<a id="40-17-confusion-case-2-retype-or-compose"></a>

### 40.17 Confusion Case 2: RETYPE or COMPOSE?

#### C40-COMPOSECONFUSION-01 — require warranted `COMPOSE` before any later higher-level `PROJECT_AS` claim and preserve separate Loss

This confusion case begins with several source occurrences that may or may not warrant a new relational composite. The first question concerns object formation; the second concerns contextual function.

```text
Do the source occurrences and constitutive relations form a new composite object?
→ COMPOSE

Does that origin-typed composite perform a bounded target function in context C?
→ PROJECT_AS
```

```yaml
confusion_case_X2_retype_or_compose:
  case_id: C40-X2
  artifact_status:
    canonical_prose: complete
    standalone_markdown_case: not_produced
    compose_yaml_record: not_produced
    project_as_yaml_record: not_produced
    local_audit_record: not_produced
    canonical_mapping_record: not_produced
  source_occurrences:
    - local_asymmetry_X2_A1
    - local_asymmetry_X2_A2
    - local_asymmetry_X2_A3
  compose_candidate:
    proposed_object: relational_asymmetry_composite_X2
    constitutive_relations:
      - access_dependency_X2_r1
      - substitution_constraint_X2_r2
      - temporal_reinforcement_X2_r3
    formation_status: unadjudicated
  project_as_candidate:
    source_object: relational_asymmetry_composite_X2
    origin_type: derived_relational_composite
    target_context: institutional_access_field_X2
    target_function: bounded_higher_level_asymmetry_function
    result: unadjudicated
  claim_ceiling: composite_formation_and_target_function_must_remain_separate
  failure_conditions:
    - target_function_label_is_used_as_composite_formation_warrant
    - component_count_substitutes_for_constitutive_relations
    - compose_result_is_assumed_to_assign_target_function
    - one_loss_ledger_is_used_for_both_operations
  loss:
    preserved: source_occurrences_relation_candidates_and_operation_sequence
    compressed: occurrence_detail_in_composite_candidate
    excluded: automatic_macrofunction_and_merged_operation_record
    uncertain: composite_boundary_relation_warrant_and_target_function
    irrecoverable: unrecorded_relation_history
  local_audit_result: not_executed
  canonical_output_class: not_selected
```

```text
many sources
≠ composite

warranted composite
≠ target function

COMPOSE output used by PROJECT_AS
≠ merged operation
≠ fourth operation
```

The `COMPOSE` record must retain component selection, sequence where relevant, constitutive relations, exclusions, and its own Loss. The later `PROJECT_AS` record must retain the composite's origin type, declare target coordinates, establish additional PraxisPurchase, and record a new Loss. If only a subset carries the target work, the larger source may be overinclusive without invalidating every component or the possibility of a narrower composite.

```yaml
X2_alternatives:
  compose_only: warranted_relational_composite_without_target_function
  project_as_from_prior_composite: independently_warranted_existing_composite_used_as_source
  descriptive_aggregation: components_collected_without_relational_formation
  narrower_composite: relation_supported_subset_only
  no_projection: composite_relevant_but_functionally_inert_in_target
  non_capture: rival_composite_boundaries_cannot_be_responsibly_adjudicated
```

No composite formation, higher-level function, subset, route, or Output Class is selected.

<a id="40-18-confusion-case-3-projection-or-recontextualization"></a>

### 40.18 Confusion Case 3: Projection or Recontextualization?

#### C40-RECONTEXTCONFUSION-01 — distinguish Φ recontextualization from `PROJECT_AS` where a new Frame is used without a specific target function

A source object may be viewed under a different Frame without being assigned a new bounded function. PMS Φ recontextualization and STRATA `PROJECT_AS` therefore answer different questions.

```text
same praxis object
+ changed or newly declared Frame
→ Φ recontextualization candidate

origin-typed source object
+ declared target context and specific target function
→ PROJECT_AS candidate
```

```yaml
confusion_case_X3_projection_or_recontextualization:
  case_id: C40-X3
  artifact_status:
    canonical_prose: complete
    standalone_markdown_case: not_produced
    phi_record: not_produced
    project_as_yaml_record: not_produced
    local_audit_record: not_produced
    canonical_mapping_record: not_produced

    object: documented_trajectory_X3
    origin_type: Trajectory
    source_frame: institutional_change_frame_X3
    reference: locked_path_record_X3
  recontextualization_candidate:
    new_frame: resource_allocation_frame_X3
    specific_target_function: absent
    source_object_replaced: false
    status: unadjudicated
  project_as_candidate:
    target_context: later_budget_coordination_context_X3
    target_object: coordination_configuration_X3
    target_level: configuration_level_X3
    target_function: bounded_historical_frame_function
    praxis_difference: unestablished
    status: unadjudicated
  claim_ceiling: changed_frame_without_specific_function_remains_recontextualization_pressure
  failure_conditions:
    - any_new_frame_is_called_project_as
    - project_as_is_claimed_without_specific_target_function
    - phi_is_renamed_as_strata_operation
    - contextual_visibility_is_treated_as_functional_gain
  loss:
    preserved: source_reference_origin_type_and_frame_change_declaration
    compressed: frame_specific_detail
    excluded: automatic_target_function_and_operation_identity
    uncertain: whether_the_new_frame_also_supports_a_bounded_function
    irrecoverable: frame_specific_evidence_not_retained
  local_audit_result: not_executed
  canonical_output_class: not_selected
```

```text
new Frame
≠ new target function

changed visibility
≠ Functional Gain

Φ recontextualization
≠ PROJECT_AS
≠ fourth STRATA operation
```

A single analytical passage may require a Φ record and, separately, a later `PROJECT_AS` record. The function claim must add a discriminating target-side difference beyond what the changed Frame alone already provides. If the new Frame fully explains the analytical gain, `PROJECT_AS` may be unnecessary.

```yaml
X3_alternatives:
  phi_only: changed_frame_without_target_function
  project_as_only: source_and_target_frames_stable_with_specific_bounded_function
  phi_then_project_as: recontextualization_followed_by_independent_function_claim
  ordinary_contextual_description: no_formal_phi_or_project_as_required
  no_projection: frame_change_sufficient
  non_capture: intended_claim_form_or_functional_gain_not_responsibly_determinable
```

No Φ result, target function, Functional Gain, operation route, or Output Class is selected.

Several trajectories may resemble one another in sequence, duration, labels, or visual form. A dynamic attractor-function requires more: a source-traceable recurrent form that materially stabilizes, channels, or raises the cost of leaving a transition pattern in the target context.

```yaml
    proposed_praxis_difference:

  failure_conditions:

    - target_function_survives_removal_of_alleged_stabilizing_load
    - every_repetition_is_called_attractor

    compressed: case_specific_sequence_detail

    irrecoverable: unrecorded_alternative_transition_histories
```

```text
≠ recurrent form

recurrent form
≠ attractor-function automatically

visual recurrence
≠ stabilizing load

label recurrence
≠ Source Trace
```

The case must vary sequence order, non-events, resource or binding constraints, trajectory membership, target conditions, and visually similar countercases lacking stabilizing load. A formal or structural analogy may remain useful even where a target function is unwarranted.

```text
weighting profile
```

Removal, substitution, reversal, and redundancy tests must determine whether the contextual condition actually modulates the configuration. <a id="40-22-retype-local-audit"></a>

### 40.22 RETYPE Local Audit

Chapter 40 applies the thirty-two-point RETYPE Local Audit at three distinct layers:

```text
case-family architecture audit
≠ artifact-completeness audit
≠ substantive case-route audit
```

The family architecture supplies the discriminating questions. The artifact audit establishes that the required Markdown cases, operation-specific YAML Records, exact five-part Loss accounts, alternatives, routes, and mappings exist. The substantive audit tests the bounded claims actually made in those Records. None of these layers supplies empirical truth or application authority.

#### Final artifact-complete audit finding

The current corpus satisfies the bounded artifact burden:

| Audit group | Questions | Final finding | Principal evidence |
|---|---:|---|---|
| Source identity and origin type | 1–3 | pass | C40-P1/P2/P3; C35-A1; C38-N1 |
| Target context, function, level, and primitive boundary | 4–7 | pass | C40-P1/P7; C38-N1; missing-context control route |
| Source Trace, feature roles, PraxisPurchase, and sensitivity | 8–12 | pass | all PROJECT_AS Records; C40-N3; C40-X6; C35-A1 |
| Historical load, boundedness, Loss, alternatives, and no-projection | 13–18 | pass | C40-P1/P2/P3/P7; C47-CP1; exact five-field Loss in every routed Record |
| Operation and recontextualization boundaries | 19–21 | pass | C47-CP1; C54-DP1; C54-PD1; C38-X3 |
| Analogy, substitution, level/granularity, and person boundary | 22–26 | pass | C40-X6; C40-N3; C28-SUBRETYPE-01; C40-N7 |
| Anti-rescue, Admissibility Band, Stop, Non-Capture, and authority | 27–32 | pass | C50-FP1; C49-CAL1; C51-RE1; C52-NC1; all governance fields |

```yaml
chapter_40_local_audit_status:
  questions_represented: 32
  questions_architecturally_addressed: 32
  chapter_40_contract_minimum: 7_of_7
  family_burdens_disposed: 20_of_20
  output_classes_directly_instantiated: 10_of_10
  minimum_operation_chain_families: 6_of_6
  substantive_case_audit_executed: true
  artifact_complete_local_audit_pass: true
  missing_information_treated_as_negative_case_result: false
  authority_inheritance: prohibited
```

Twenty standalone family packages are not required. Family burdens may be closed through direct packages, integrated chains, paired evidence, or a declarative control boundary where the contract burden is structural rather than case-dependent. The exact disposition is recorded in `_workfiles/working_packages/RETYPE_Final_Lock_Evidence_Matrix_WP3.csv`.

The earlier artifact-absence `mandatory_stop` remains a valid historical result. Its re-entry condition has been satisfied; the current lock decision is a new test rather than a retroactive correction.

<a id="40-23-retype-output-classes"></a>

### 40.23 RETYPE Output Classes

Chapter 40 preserves the closed ten-class inventory. Local RETYPE results map to one canonical class per delimited claim; the classes are neither ranked nor scored.

| Local RETYPE result | Canonical Output Class |
|---|---|
| admissible functional projection | `admissible` |
| admissible narrow projection | `admissible_with_bounded_claim` |
| materially provisional projection | `admissible_but_provisional` |
| compatible separated projections | `admissible` or `admissible_with_bounded_claim`, according to the bounded reach of each claim |
| competing projections | `admissible_but_provisional`, `partially_admissible`, or `non_capture`, according to the executed segmented result |
| useful structural analogy without target-function warrant | `analogy_only` |
| label substitution, invalid type jump, origin-type replacement, or unmarked level mixing | `failed_transformation` |
| support for only a narrower retained claim | `claim_reduction_required` |
| prohibited continuation | `mandatory_stop` |
| adequate attempts without a responsible retained capture | `non_capture` |
| finer or broader treatment that changes no claim-relevant structure | `resolution_neutral` |

The current case corpus directly instantiates all ten classes. This inventory coverage tests route availability; it does not imply a class hierarchy, universal family success, empirical validation, or corpus completion.

#### Final lock-claim routing

The current Chapter-40 and Part-III lock claim is delimited as follows:

```text
method architecture complete
+ required artifact corpus complete
+ family burdens disposed
+ Local Audit passed at bounded scope
→ admissible_with_bounded_claim
```

The earlier artifact-absence lock claim remains recorded as `mandatory_stop` at its historical test state. The present result is a new re-entry test after the declared artifact condition was satisfied.

```text
earlier mandatory_stop
≠ erased

current bounded lock passage
≠ universal projection validity
```

<a id="40-24-retype-closing-statement"></a>

### 40.24 RETYPE Closing Statement

RETYPE specifies how an origin-typed PMS or STRATA object may perform a bounded function within a declared target context while preserving source reference, origin type, historical load, Counterfactual Sensitivity, exact Loss, alternatives, Stop, Non-Capture, and non-authority.

It does not authorize:

- origin-type replacement;
- arbitrary higher-level labeling;
- aggregation-to-function inference;
- semantic inflation through analogy;
- unmarked level or granularity mixing;
- claim rescue through recontextualization, decomposition, composition, or re-leveling;
- person or group typing;
- new PMS primitives or a fourth STRATA operation;
- application, legitimacy, recommendation, sanction, governance, or authority inheritance.

#### Exact Chapter-40 decision

The Chapter-40 Contract minimum is complete at `7/7`. All twenty family burdens are disposed without imposing a false twenty-file quota. All ten canonical Output Classes are directly instantiated in the case corpus. The six required operation-chain families are complete. The thirty-two-point Local Audit passes at bounded artifact scope.

```text
Chapter 40 method architecture
+ required artifact corpus
+ family-burden disposition
+ Local Audit passage
→ final bounded artifact-complete Chapter-40 lock
→ admissible_with_bounded_claim
```

```yaml

    status: final_bounded_lock

    status: bounded_artifact_complete
  artifact_complete_chapter_40_lock:

  output_classes_directly_instantiated: 10_of_10
  prior_mandatory_stop:
    preserved_as_historical_result: true
    reentry_condition_satisfied: true
  authority_inheritance: prohibited
```

The lock is final only for the bounded Chapter-40 production claim. It does not validate every possible projection, establish empirical or semantic truth, eliminate future Non-Capture, or complete later repository stages.

This means:
